cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(stencil3d_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(stencil3d stencil3d.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;

    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) / 7.0;
         }
     }
 }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values

+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) / 7.0;
         }
     }

+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,

The CUDA implementation is in place, including fixed boundaries, odd/even buffer handling, and bounded graph batches for long runs. I’m building it now, then I’ll compare its full-grid hashes with the original CPU executable before tuning the kernels.

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

struct CudaResources {
    Real* grid1 = nullptr;
    Real* grid2 = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;

    ~CudaResources() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
        if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
        if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
        if (grid1) cudaFree(grid1);
        if (grid2) cudaFree(grid2);
    }
};

__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ grid1,
                               Real* __restrict__ grid2, size_t count) {
    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < count; i += stride) {
        const Real value = Real(i % 19);
        // Boundaries are identical in both buffers and never written again.
        grid1[i] = value;
        grid2[i] = value;
    }
}

constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;

// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;

    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
            Real center = input[i];
            Real top = input[i + plane];

            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
                const Real left = input[i - 1];
                const Real right = input[i + 1];
                const Real front = input[i - nx];
                const Real back = input[i + nx];
                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
                bottom = center;
                center = top;
                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
                i += plane;
            }
        }
    }
}

// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < count; i += stride) {
        const size_t x = i % nx;
        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) / 7.0;
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;

    const size_t maxElements = std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(Real);
    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 || iterations < 0 ||
        nx > maxElements / ny || nx * ny > maxElements / nz) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid grid dimensions or iteration count\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);
    CudaResources gpu;
    int device = 0;
    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&gpu.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&gpu.start));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&gpu.stop));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.grid1, bytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.grid2, bytes));
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    const unsigned linearBlocks = unsigned(std::min(
        (gridSize + 255) / 256, size_t(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32));
    initializeGrid<<<linearBlocks, 256, 0, gpu.stream>>>(gpu.grid1, gpu.grid2, gridSize);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

    const bool hasInterior = nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2;
    const bool useLinear = nx < TileX || ny < TileY;
    dim3 blocks(1, 1, 1);
    if (hasInterior) {
        blocks = dim3(unsigned((nx - 1 + TileX - 1) / TileX),
                      unsigned(std::min((ny - 2 + TileY - 1) / TileY,
                                        size_t(properties.maxGridSize[1]))),
                      unsigned(std::min((nz - 2 + TileZ - 1) / TileZ,
                                        size_t(properties.maxGridSize[2]))));
    }

    auto enqueueIteration = [&](int iter) {
        const Real* input = (iter % 2 == 0) ? gpu.grid1 : gpu.grid2;
        Real* output = (iter % 2 == 0) ? gpu.grid2 : gpu.grid1;
        if (useLinear) {
            stencilIterationLinear<<<linearBlocks, 256, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
                input, output, nx, ny, gridSize);
        } else {
            stencilIteration<<<blocks, dim3(TileX, TileY), 0, gpu.stream>>>(
                input, output, nx, ny, nz);
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    };

    // Capture at most 64 iterations to bound graph construction/storage for
    // long runs. Repeated batches are even-sized, preserving buffer parity.
    const int batch = std::min(iterations, 64);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
    if (hasInterior && batch > 0) {
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(gpu.stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
        for (int iter = 0; iter < batch; ++iter) enqueueIteration(iter);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(gpu.stream, &gpu.graph));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&gpu.executable, gpu.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(gpu.executable, gpu.stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
    }
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.start, gpu.stream));
    if (hasInterior && batch > 0) {
        const int batches = iterations / batch;
        for (int replay = 0; replay < batches; ++replay)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(gpu.executable, gpu.stream));
        for (int iter = batches * batch; iter < iterations; ++iter)
            enqueueIteration(iter);
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.stop, gpu.stream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.stop));
    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, gpu.start, gpu.stop));

    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", double(milliseconds));
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const double cellUpdates = hasInterior ?
        double(nx - 2) * double(ny - 2) * double(nz - 2) * iterations : 0.0;
    const double mcups = milliseconds > 0 ? cellUpdates / (milliseconds * 1000.0) : 0.0;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        finalGrid.resize(gridSize);
        const Real* finalDeviceGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? gpu.grid1 : gpu.grid2;
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(finalGrid.data(), finalDeviceGrid, bytes,
                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, gpu.stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
    }
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)

# Target the installed GPU by default; callers can specify architectures for
# cross-compilation with -DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=... .
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
endif()
project(stencil3d_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 17)
set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

# Keep the existing filename and executable name while compiling with nvcc.
set_source_files_properties(stencil3d.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
add_executable(stencil3d stencil3d.cpp)

# Preserve double-precision arithmetic without fast-math reassociation.
target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE
    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3>
    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:--fmad=false>
    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-Xcompiler=-O3,-march=native,-Wall,-Wextra>)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values

+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) / 7.0;
         }
     }
 }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)

+                i += plane;

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

struct CudaResources {
    Real* grid1 = nullptr;
    Real* grid2 = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;

    ~CudaResources() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
        if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
        if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
        if (grid1) cudaFree(grid1);
        if (grid2) cudaFree(grid2);
    }
};

__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ grid1,
                               Real* __restrict__ grid2, size_t count) {
    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < count; i += stride) {
        const Real value = Real(i % 19);
        // Boundaries are identical in both buffers and never written again.
        grid1[i] = value;
        grid2[i] = value;
    }
}

constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;

// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;

    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
            Real center = input[i];
            Real top = input[i + plane];

            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
                const Real left = input[i - 1];
                const Real right = input[i + 1];
                const Real front = input[i - nx];
                const Real back = input[i + nx];
                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
                bottom = center;
                center = top;
                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
                i += plane;
            }
        }
    }
}

// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < count; i += stride) {
        const size_t x = i % nx;
        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) / 7.0;
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;

    const size_t maxElements = std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(Real);
    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 || iterations < 0 ||
        nx > maxElements / ny || nx * ny > maxElements / nz) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid grid dimensions or iteration count\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);
    CudaResources gpu;
    int device = 0;
    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&gpu.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&gpu.start));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&gpu.stop));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.grid1, bytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.grid2, bytes));
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    const unsigned linearBlocks = unsigned(std::min(
        (gridSize + 255) / 256, size_t(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32));
    initializeGrid<<<linearBlocks, 256, 0, gpu.stream>>>(gpu.grid1, gpu.grid2, gridSize);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

    const bool hasInterior = nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2;
    const bool useLinear = nx < TileX || ny < TileY;
    dim3 blocks(1, 1, 1);
    if (hasInterior) {
        blocks = dim3(unsigned((nx - 1 + TileX - 1) / TileX),
                      unsigned(std::min((ny - 2 + TileY - 1) / TileY,
                                        size_t(properties.maxGridSize[1]))),
                      unsigned(std::min((nz - 2 + TileZ - 1) / TileZ,
                                        size_t(properties.maxGridSize[2]))));
    }

    auto enqueueIteration = [&](int iter) {
        const Real* input = (iter % 2 == 0) ? gpu.grid1 : gpu.grid2;
        Real* output = (iter % 2 == 0) ? gpu.grid2 : gpu.grid1;
        if (useLinear) {
            stencilIterationLinear<<<linearBlocks, 256, 0, gpu.stream>>>(
                input, output, nx, ny, gridSize);
        } else {
            stencilIteration<<<blocks, dim3(TileX, TileY), 0, gpu.stream>>>(
                input, output, nx, ny, nz);
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    };

    // Capture at most 64 iterations to bound graph construction/storage for
    // long runs. Repeated batches are even-sized, preserving buffer parity.
    const int batch = std::min(iterations, 64);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
    if (hasInterior && batch > 0) {
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(gpu.stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
        for (int iter = 0; iter < batch; ++iter) enqueueIteration(iter);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(gpu.stream, &gpu.graph));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&gpu.executable, gpu.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(gpu.executable, gpu.stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
    }
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.start, gpu.stream));
    if (hasInterior && batch > 0) {
        const int batches = iterations / batch;
        for (int replay = 0; replay < batches; ++replay)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(gpu.executable, gpu.stream));
        for (int iter = batches * batch; iter < iterations; ++iter)
            enqueueIteration(iter);
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.stop, gpu.stream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.stop));
    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, gpu.start, gpu.stop));

    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", double(milliseconds));
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const double cellUpdates = hasInterior ?
        double(nx - 2) * double(ny - 2) * double(nz - 2) * iterations : 0.0;
    const double mcups = milliseconds > 0 ? cellUpdates / (milliseconds * 1000.0) : 0.0;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        finalGrid.resize(gridSize);
        const Real* finalDeviceGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? gpu.grid1 : gpu.grid2;
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(finalGrid.data(), finalDeviceGrid, bytes,
                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, gpu.stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
    }
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values

+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) / 7.0;
         }
     }

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import os, subprocess
from pathlib import Path
source=Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp').read_text().split('void printUsage')[0]
source=source.replace('\"../common/results_output.hpp\"','\"'+str(Path('common/results_output.hpp').resolve())+'\"')
source=source.replace('constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;','').replace('constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;','')
source=source.replace('__global__ void stencilIteration(', 'template<unsigned TileY, unsigned TileZ>\\n__global__ void stencilIteration(')
source+=r'''
template<unsigned Y, unsigned Z>
void measure(CudaResources& gpu, unsigned n, unsigned iterations) {
    dim3 blocks((n-1+31)/32,(n-2+Y-1)/Y,(n-2+Z-1)/Z);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(gpu.stream,cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
    for (unsigned i=0;i<iterations;++i) {
        stencilIteration<Y,Z><<<blocks,dim3(32,Y),0,gpu.stream>>>(i%2?gpu.grid2:gpu.grid1,i%2?gpu.grid1:gpu.grid2,n,n,n);
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(gpu.stream,&gpu.graph));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&gpu.executable,gpu.graph,nullptr,nullptr,0));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(gpu.executable,gpu.stream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.start,gpu.stream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(gpu.executable,gpu.stream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.stop,gpu.stream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.stop));
    float time;CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&time,gpu.start,gpu.stop));
    printf(\"%u Y%u Z%u: %.3f ms %.1f Gcells/s\\n\",n,Y,Z,time,double(n-2)*(n-2)*(n-2)*iterations/time/1e6);
    fflush(stdout);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(gpu.executable));gpu.executable=nullptr;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(gpu.graph));gpu.graph=nullptr;
}
int main(){
  for(unsigned n: {128,256,512}) {
    CudaResources gpu;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&gpu.stream,cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&gpu.start));CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&gpu.stop));
    size_t count=size_t(n)*n*n;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.grid1,count*8));CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&gpu.grid2,count*8));
    initializeGrid<<<2048,256,0,gpu.stream>>>(gpu.grid1,gpu.grid2,count);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(gpu.stream));
    measure<4,1>(gpu,n,40);measure<4,2>(gpu,n,40);measure<4,4>(gpu,n,40);
    measure<4,8>(gpu,n,40);measure<4,16>(gpu,n,40);
    measure<8,1>(gpu,n,40);measure<8,2>(gpu,n,40);measure<8,4>(gpu,n,40);
    measure<8,8>(gpu,n,40);measure<8,16>(gpu,n,40);
    measure<16,2>(gpu,n,40);measure<16,4>(gpu,n,40);measure<16,8>(gpu,n,40);
  }
}
'''
fd=os.memfd_create('stencil3d_tuning')
os.write(fd,source.encode())

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ grid1,
+                               Real* __restrict__ grid2, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const Real value = Real(i % 19);
+        // Boundaries are identical in both buffers and never written again.
+        grid1[i] = value;
+        grid2[i] = value;
     }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) / 7.0;
         }
     }
 }

+struct CudaResources {

+    Real* grid2 = nullptr;

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ grid1,
+                               Real* __restrict__ grid2, size_t count) {

-

-                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) / 7.0;
         }
     }
 }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) / 7.0;
         }
     }
 }

+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. For these finite, normal sums (or zero),
+    // correct reciprocal rounding with an exact FMA residual. Division by the
+    // odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie; the correction rounds to the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 without CUDA's general-purpose division path.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = sum * reciprocal;
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
 }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ grid1,
+                               Real* __restrict__ grid2, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {

+        // Boundaries are identical in both buffers and never written again.

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;

+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. For these finite, normal sums (or zero),
+    // correct reciprocal rounding with an exact FMA residual. Division by the
+    // odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie; the correction rounds to the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 without CUDA's general-purpose division path.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = sum * reciprocal;
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
 }

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }

+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,

+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;

+                                     input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +

import os, subprocess
from pathlib import Path
source=Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp').read_text().split('void printUsage')[0]
source=source.replace('\"../common/results_output.hpp\"','\"'+str(Path('common/results_output.hpp').resolve())+'\"')
source+=r'''
__global__ void checkAverage(unsigned* errors, size_t count) {
  for(size_t i=size_t(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;i<count;i+=size_t(blockDim.x)*gridDim.x) {
    unsigned long long mantissa=i*0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    mantissa"'^=mantissa>>30;mantissa*=0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
    mantissa''^=mantissa>>27;mantissa*=0x94d049bb133111ebULL;mantissa''^=mantissa>>31;
    unsigned long long bits=((16ULL+i%1014)<<52)|(mantissa&0xfffffffffffffULL);
    double sum=i==0?0:__longlong_as_double(bits);
    double expected=__ddiv_rn(sum,7.0);
    double actual=averageSeven(sum);
    if(__double_as_longlong(expected)!=__double_as_longlong(actual)) atomicAdd(errors,1);
  }
}

  unsigned host;CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&host,errors,sizeof(unsigned),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
  printf("Checked 16777216 normal sums and zero: %u mismatches'"\\n\",host);
  CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(errors));return host?1:0;
}
'''

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ grid1,

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. For these finite, normal sums (or zero),
+    // correct reciprocal rounding with an exact FMA residual. Division by the
+    // odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie; the correction rounds to the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 without CUDA's general-purpose division path.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = sum * reciprocal;
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }

void measure(CudaResources& gpu, unsigned n, unsigned iterations) {
