+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }

-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }

+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                     input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                     input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. For these finite, normal sums (or zero),
+    // correct reciprocal rounding with an exact FMA residual. Division by the
+    // odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie; the correction rounds to the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 without CUDA's general-purpose division path.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = sum * reciprocal;
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+    if (sum >= 0x1p-960)
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+
+    // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual underflow
+    // if exceptionally long runs drive an interior value into this range.
+    const Real quotient = sum * reciprocal;
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }

+                                     input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                     input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+    if (sum >= 0x1p-960)
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+
+    // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual underflow
+    // if exceptionally long runs drive an interior value into this range.
+    const Real quotient = sum * reciprocal;
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {

+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still

+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;

+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {

+                                     input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                     input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);

     }

__global__ void checkAverage(unsigned* errors, size_t count) {
  for(size_t i=size_t(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;i<count;i+=size_t(blockDim.x)*gridDim.x) {
    unsigned long long mantissa=i*0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    mantissa"'^=mantissa>>30;mantissa*=0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
    mantissa''^=mantissa>>27;mantissa*=0x94d049bb133111ebULL;mantissa''^=mantissa>>31;
    unsigned long long bits=((i%1030)<<52)|(mantissa&0xfffffffffffffULL);
    if(i<1048576) bits=i;
    double sum=__longlong_as_double(bits);
    double expected=__ddiv_rn(sum,7.0);
    double actual=averageSeven(sum);
    if(__double_as_longlong(expected)!=__double_as_longlong(actual)) atomicAdd(errors,1);
  }
}

  CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(errors,0,sizeof(unsigned)));

  unsigned host;CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&host,errors,sizeof(unsigned),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
  printf("Checked 16777216 sums including zero and subnormals: %u mismatches'"\\n\",host);
  CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(errors));return host?1:0;
}

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+    if (sum >= 0x1p-960)
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+
+    // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual underflow
+    // if exceptionally long runs drive an interior value into this range.
+    const Real quotient = sum * reciprocal;
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * TileZ + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * TileZ) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < TileZ; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < TileZ && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                     input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                     input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+    if (sum >= 0x1p-960)
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+
+    // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual underflow
+    // if exceptionally long runs drive an interior value into this range.
+    const Real quotient = sum * reciprocal;
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+template<unsigned Z>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * Z + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * Z) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < Z; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < Z && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                     input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                     input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ grid1,

-void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+    if (sum >= 0x1p-960)
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+
+    // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual underflow
+    // if exceptionally long runs drive an interior value into this range.
+    const Real quotient = sum * reciprocal;
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+template<unsigned Z>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * Z + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * Z) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < Z; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < Z && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                     input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                     input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }

I’m using those results to select the final kernels, then I’ll rerun correctness and sanitizer checks on the final build.

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ grid1,
+                               Real* __restrict__ grid2, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    if constexpr (BoundedRange) {
+        constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+    } else {
+        // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual
+        // underflow during exceptionally long runs.
+        const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+        const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+        return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
     }
 }

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+template<unsigned Z, bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * Z + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * Z) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < Z; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < Z && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                                  input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                                  input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }
 }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;

+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    if constexpr (BoundedRange) {
+        constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+    } else {
+        // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual
+        // underflow during exceptionally long runs.
+        const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+        const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+        return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
     }
 }

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+template<unsigned Z, bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * Z + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * Z) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < Z; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < Z && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-

+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;

     }

__global__ void checkAverage(unsigned* errors, size_t count) {
  for(size_t i=size_t(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;i<count;i+=size_t(blockDim.x)*gridDim.x) {
    unsigned long long mantissa=i*0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    mantissa"'^=mantissa>>30;mantissa*=0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ grid1,
+                               Real* __restrict__ grid2, size_t count) {
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {

+        // Boundaries are identical in both buffers and never written again.
+        grid1[i] = value;

+    }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned TileZ = 8;
+
+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    if constexpr (BoundedRange) {
+        constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+    } else {
+        // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual
+        // underflow during exceptionally long runs.
+        const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+        const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+        return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
     }
 }

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+template<unsigned Z, bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * Z + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * Z) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < Z; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < Z && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-

-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                                  input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                                  input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }

+template<unsigned Z, bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * Z + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * Z) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < Z; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < Z && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                                  input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                                  input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }

+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    if constexpr (BoundedRange) {
+        constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+    } else {
+        // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual
+        // underflow during exceptionally long runs.
+        const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+        const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+        return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
     }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;

+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    if constexpr (BoundedRange) {
+        constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+    } else {
+        // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual
+        // underflow during exceptionally long runs.
+        const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+        const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+        return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
     }
 }

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+template<unsigned Z, bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * Z + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * Z) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < Z; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < Z && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                                  input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                                  input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }

+constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned TileY = 4;

+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real averageSeven(Real sum) {
+    // Grid values stay in [0, 18]. A two-double reciprocal approximates 1/7
+    // to 106 bits; the FMA rounds its product just once. Since division by
+    // the odd integer 7 cannot produce a halfway tie, this gives exactly the
+    // same double as sum / 7.0 throughout the normal range used here.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    if constexpr (BoundedRange) {
+        constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+    } else {
+        // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual
+        // underflow during exceptionally long runs.
+        const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+        const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+        return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
     }
 }

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+template<unsigned Z, bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * Z + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * Z) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < Z; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < Z && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-

-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}

+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                                  input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                                  input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }

+        constexpr Real reciprocalTail = 0x1.2492492492492p-57;
+        return __fma_rn(sum, reciprocal, sum * reciprocalTail);
+    } else {
+        // An exact residual correction also handles zero and gradual
+        // underflow during exceptionally long runs.
+        const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+        const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, sum);
+        return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
     }
 }

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A thread advances through
+// several Z planes, keeping the center and Z neighbors in registers.
+template<unsigned Z, bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * TileX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x == 0 || x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Grid strides also support thin grids exceeding CUDA's Y/Z grid limits.
+    for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * TileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+         y < ny - 1; y += size_t(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
+        for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * Z + 1;
+             firstZ < nz - 1; firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * Z) {
+            size_t i = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[i - plane];
+            Real center = input[i];
+            Real top = input[i + plane];
+
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned dz = 0; dz < Z; ++dz) {
+                if (firstZ + dz >= nz - 1) break;
+                const Real left = input[i - 1];
+                const Real right = input[i + 1];
+                const Real front = input[i - nx];
+                const Real back = input[i + nx];
+                output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                if (dz + 1 < Z && firstZ + dz + 1 < nz - 1)
+                    top = input[i + 2 * plane];
+                i += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+}
+
+// Avoid mostly inactive warps for narrow grids; neighboring threads still
+// access neighboring elements of the original, unpadded grid.
+template<bool BoundedRange>
+__global__ void stencilIterationLinear(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                       Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                       size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t count) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t stride = size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += stride) {
+        const size_t x = i % nx;
+        const size_t y = (i / nx) % ny;
+        if (i >= plane && i < count - plane &&
+            x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y > 0 && y < ny - 1) {
+            output[i] = averageSeven<BoundedRange>(input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                                                  input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                                                  input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]);
         }
     }
 }

+    auto enqueueIteration = [&](int iter) {

+                    input, output, nx, ny, gridSize);
